| Links |  |  |  |

| A golem made out of pure Nachore! Their magmatic stomach bile melts the Nachore down into a more refined, molten liquid that circulates throughout their body. They excrete the byproduct of the digested Nachore through their pores which forms their Nachore-like exterior. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 932 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Nachore Golem Mouse has been found to be attracted to Medium Queso.

### Charm

NA

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | Shadow | None |

## Hunting Strategy

Only Shadow traps are effective against the Nachore Golem Mouse, all other power types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 26 June 2018: The Nachore Golem Mouse was introduced as part of the Queso Canyon release

| The unchallenged lord of the Nachore underworld that exists deep below Cantera Quarry, Nachous is powerful and pulsing with molten blood, able to mould his body into whatever form is necessary. He is fabled to possess the legendary Ember Stone, giving him great power and control over heat and the earth itself.<br>Never seen or heard but his name is whispered on the lips of those who dare to dig too deep. Satisfied by only the hottest Nachore closest to the core of Gnawnia, it would take something extremely hot to grab this great one's attention...<br>This mouse is only interested in Flamin' Queso. No other cheese will do! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 933 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Nachous the Molten is only attracted to Flamin' Queso in Cantera Quarry.

Shadow power type is very effective against this mouse.
All other types are ineffective.

# 🐭 Naturalist Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Naturalist Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Whisker Woods Rift.

| Naturalist Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Rift Walkers (Whisker Woods Rift) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 85,000 | Gold: | 21,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Rift | Other Requirements: | 0-50 Rage in Gigantic Gnarled Tree |
| Cheese: | Marble StringSwiss StringBrie StringMagical String | Charm: | Gnarled Charm |
| Locations: | Whisker Woods Rift | Loot: | Flawless OrbRift CherriesSap-filled ThornsSimple Orb |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Hip and in the now, wearing the latest in all-natural growth wear, he sports the tastiest natural growth potions around! This nature-loving scientist works diligently on his all-natural organic products and concoctions. Guaranteed to bring you one step closer to nature than you thought possible! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 691 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Naturalist mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice. They also will not appear unless a Gnarled Charm is armed.

## Hunting Strategy

The Naturalist Mouse is weak to Rift Traps. The Naturalist Mouse can only be attracted by String Cheese and Gnarled Charm. Catching the Naturalist Mouse will award 6 Rage towards the Gigantic Gnarled Tree section of the Whisker Woods Rift.

The Naturalist Mouse is available at any rage in Whisker Woods Rift.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Naturalist Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg and the Naturalist Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Naturalist mice were introduced on 28 January 2015, with the introduction of the Whisker Woods Rift.
- Naturalist mice are the Rift version of Curious Chemist mice.

# 🐭 Nerg Lich Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Nerg Lich Mouse is a breed of mouse found in Balack's Cove. It is known to drop Flawless Orb and Raisins of Wrath as loot. It is also known to drop the Lich Jewel as loot, but only while hunters are in the process of completing the Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.

| Nerg Lich Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Balack's Banished |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 40,144 | Gold: | 10,340 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | ForgottenArcaneShadow | Other Requirements: |  |
| Cheese: | Vengeful Vanilla StiltonVanilla Stilton | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Balack's Cove | Loot: | Flawless OrbLich JewelRaisins of WrathScrap Metal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| In life, the Nerg Elders prided themselves on their speed and agility. Because the Nerg place such a high value on skillful fighting moves, Balack devised the perfect immortal punishment for them-- strapping heavier and heavier weights on their bodies to limit their movement. The once nimble Nerg elders now shamble about, swinging their weights as weapons rather than exercising their fighting skill. They may have lost their one joy in life, but they still have plenty of wrath. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 140 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Nerg Lich Mouse has been found to be particularly attracted to Vengeful Vanilla Stilton and, to a lesser extent, Vanilla Stilton cheeses.

## History and Trivia

- 17 May 2010: The Nerg Lich Mouse is released with the introduction of Balack's Cove.
- 14 April 2011: The Nerg Lich Mouse began dropping a bonus Cove Egg as loot during the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 23 August 2011: The Nerg Lich Mouse began to drop the Lich Jewel as loot.
- 6 February 2014: The Nerg Lich Mouse began to drop Flawless Orbs as loot.
- 22 March 2022: The Nerg Lich Mouse began to drop Scrap Metal as loot.

# 🐭 New Year's Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The New Year's Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual Great Winter Hunt and Lunar New Year events.

| New Year's Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (New Year) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 10,000 | Gold: | 2,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All types | Other Requirements: | See current Great Winter Huntor Lunar New Year location |
| Cheese: | See current Great Winter Huntor Lunar New Year location | Charm: | See current Great Winter Huntor Lunar New Year location |
| Locations: | Great Winter HuntLunar New Year | Loot: | See current Great Winter Huntor Lunar New Year location |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This mouse is determined to ring in the New Year with a bang. After attending all of the parties it could find, it has emerged with someone else's oversized shirt, a tie that should be outlawed, and sixty pounds of fireworks which have been outlawed in most cities. The New Year's mouse intends to put on a light show so amazing that it lights up the sky for the rest of the year-- the only problem is that it hasn't really thought about how it's going to get off the ground. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 141 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the New Year's Mouse can be found on the current Great Winter Hunt or Lunar New Year location page.

## History and Trivia

- 30 December 2009: The New Year's Mouse was released for the first time in Ronza's Traveling Shoppe during The Great Winter Hunt '09 event.
- Due to a typographical error, the New Year's Mouse was originally released as the "New Year's Mouse Mouse". The release of MouseHunt Version 3.0 changed it again to "New Years Mouse", but by 2013, the missing apostrophe was restored.
- 30 December 2010: The New Year's Mouse was released with the image updated to have the firecracker labelled "2011".
- 31 December 2010: It was revealed during Feedback Friday that the King's Reserve Bubbleh loot drop was extremely rare; approximately 6,000 bottles
- 30 December 2011: The New Year's Mouse was released in the Festive Comet and across the kingdom during the Great Winter Hunt 2011, but the image remained unchanged with the firecracker still stating 2011. Outside of the Festive Comet location, it dropped regional gifts, such as the Gnawnia Winter Gift, as loot.
- 23 January 2012: The New Year's Mouse was released as part of the Year of the Dragon Festival and its image was updated with the year no longer showing on the firecracker.
- During the Extreme Toboggan Challenge, the New Year's Mouse was caught as a Bonus Hunt and did not consume cheese or charms.
- 10 December 2019: Increased point reward for New Year's Mouse

# 🐭 Nibbler Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Nibbler Mouse is a breed of mouse found in various locations in Gnawnia, Valour, Whisker Woods, Burroughs, Furoma, Tribal Isles and Rodentia .

| Nibbler Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Misc.) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,100 | Gold: | 1,400 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Any type | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | BazaarCape ClawedGreat Gnarled TreeKing's ArmsS.S. Huntington IVTournament HallTown of DigbyTown of GnawniaTraining Grounds | Loot: | Ancient Relic |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| The Nibbler Mouse is the information-gathering mouse that reports back to its fellow mice about the placement of traps, advancement of hunters, and other mouse-worthy news to help them all gain an advantage over the King's forces. This mouse is very hard to catch on a regular basis because its goal is to visit traps multiple times, hoping to gather as much intel as possible (while taking cheese samples for testing) before returning and giving a very informative and enjoyable report to those mice wanting to keep up to date. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 142 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Nibbler Mouse is attracted only to SUPER|brie+.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Nibbler Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Nibbler Mouse drops Ancient Relics in all locations where it can be found.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2009: The Nibbler Mouse was released as a thank you to the MouseHunt community. It took its name from The Nibbler, the now-defunct community run MouseHunt newspaper.
- 2 September 2009: The Nibbler Mouse began appearing in the Cape Clawed location.
- June 2010: The Nibbler Mouse was temporarily removed from the Cape Clawed location in June-July 2010 and reappeared in August 2010.
- Unknown date: The Nibbler Mouse was originally available in the Harbour and Lagoon but relocated from those locations to the Bazaar and S.S. Huntington IV.
- 14 April 2011: The Nibbler Mouse began dropping a bonus Nibbler Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 29 August 2012: The Nibbler Mouse began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Nibbler Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Nibbler Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 5 October 2021: The Gold for Nibbler Mouse was increased as part of the Town of Gnawnia and Windmill gameplay update.

| An extremely powerful mage, this mouse uses his powers to commune with the meteorite and control the phases of the night. Performing powerful rituals to bend the darkness to his will, he empowers his fellow Shadow mice by giving them more time to lay siege to your fort.<br>Failing to catch the Nightmancer Mouse allows him to complete his ritual, pushing back the dawn and extending the night by 3 hunts. |  |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Nightmancer Mouse have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Shadow traps are very effective against the Nightmancer Mouse. Arcane traps are of normal effectiveness.
The Nightmancer Mouse can only be encountered during the Twilight, Midnight, Pitch, and Utter Darkness phases at Nighttime in Fort Rox.
Once a hunter has upgraded the Fort Rox fort to the Sunsilver Ballista, it is no longer possible to miss a Nightmancer.

Upon a failure to catch a Nightmancer, the following message is displayed in the Hunter's Journal:

> The night sky darkens... Failing to catch the Nightmancer Mouse extended the night by 3 hunts.

If the hunter also has the Sunsphere Enchantment upgrade, the failure to catch message includes an additional line:

> Even the power of my Sunsphere Enchantment had no effect on this mouse!

## History and Trivia

- 3 November 2016: The Nightmancer Mouse was release as a part of Fort Rox update.

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Ninja Mouse is attracted mainly to Standard Cheese in the Mountain, Training Grounds, and Dojo locations.

In the Mountain location, the Ninja Mouse will also be attracted when hunting with White Cheddar Cheese, but is not attracted when hunting with Chedd-Ore Cheese or Abominable Asiago Cheese.
In the Dojo location, the Ninja Mouse will also be attracted when hunting with Maki Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Ninja Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | Physical | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Ninja Mouse in the Mountain, Training Grounds, and Dojo locations.

## Loot

The Ninja Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the Mountain, Training Grounds, or Dojo locations.

## History and Trivia

- The description for the Ninja Mouse has changed since its release. The original description read:

> Known for its extreme rarity, speed and intelligence the ninja mouse is one of the most challenging mice to catch. A hunter will learn much from catching such a cunning foe and is therefore worth a bundle of points.

- 14 April 2011: The Ninja Mouse began dropping the Antique Green Plaid Egg in the Training Grounds as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 16 April 2011: The Ninja Mouse began dropping the Antique Blue Argyle Egg in the Dojo as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 7 November 2017: The Ninja Mouse stopped dropping the Laboratory Map Piece as loot.

| Locations: | Town of Digby | Loot: | Ancient RelicDigby DrillBot Parts (Extremely rare)Living ShardOnyx StoneSatchel of Gold (500)Satchel of Gold (1,000)Scrap Metal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Nugget mice never grow very big, and are not very good at getting anywhere fast. When the other mice were handing out jobs, they decided the Nuggets were no competition for the likes of the Big Bad Burroughs clan. After all, Nuggets can't smash or bend or bury anything. . . and yet, the little Nuggets lead a peaceful life, gathering little bits of things that they find on the ground and hoping that they might prove useful. They're fond of shiny beetle shells, and like to form collections. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 145 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Nugget Mouse is attracted only to Limelight Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Nugget Mouse.

Arming the Mining Charm provides a power boost against Digby Dirt Dwellers.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | PhysicalShadowTacticalArcane | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Nugget Mouse only in the Town of Digby.

## Loot

- Ancient Relic
- Digby DrillBot Parts (Extremely rare)
- Living Shard
- Onyx Stone

- Satchel of Gold (1,000)
- Scrap Metal

| Mouse Group: Epilogue Ecosystem (Torrential Tyrants) |  |  |  |

| This torrential terror is as ancient and mysterious as the Epilogue Falls that it tirelessly protects. It is not entirely clear whether this aquatic aggressor is merely a guardian spirit bound to the rushing waters in this mystical place or in fact, a physical manifestation of the falls itself that lashes out angrily at trespassers. Whatever its truth and origin may be, there is one indisputable fact about this marine menace, it is a ferocious and formidable foe!<br>Hunters who successfully overcome the strongest currents of the river rapids will find themselves trapped within the Epilogue Falls and face-to-face with its last line of defence, this mighty elemental known as Nyagarha.<br>An even greater mystery than the origin of this powerful, ancient guardian is the discovery of what strange and untold secrets is it protecting... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1229 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Nyagarha the Falls Guardian is only attracted to Symbolic Sirene.

## Hunting Strategy

The Nyagarha the Falls Guardian can only be attracted within the waterfall or hidden grotto of Epilogue Falls.

Hydro power type is effective against Nyagarha the Falls Guardian.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 2 September 2025: Nyagarha the Falls Guardian was released as part of the Epilogue Falls location.

The Overcaster Mouse is a breed of mouse found on the Floating Islands, on a Shadow Island or a Shadow Stronghold.

| Overcaster Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Floating Islanders (Shadow Overcaster) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 92,000 | Gold: | 12,500 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Shadow | Other Requirements: | Shadow IslandShadow Sanctuary |
| Cheese: | Cloud CheesecakeStandard Cheese | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Floating Islands | Loot: | Sky OreSky Glass |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This sinister creature releases a concentration of shadow magic into the atmosphere which adheres to the surrounding water vapour and causes it to condense into impenetrable black clouds. These clouds can then be used as cover, transportation, or as a reservoir for storing shadow energy.<br>These mice are believed to be responsible for the notorious trojan thundercloud tactic of bygone lore. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1043 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Overcaster Mouse is attracted to Cloud Cheesecake and Standard Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Shadow traps are effective against the Overcaster Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Overcaster Mouse can only be encountered on a Shadow Island or a Shadow Sanctuary.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Island Based Loot

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Overcaster Mouse can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Overcaster Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

He is a deadly combatant and is armed to the teeth and outfitted in the finest armour and weapons the Manaforge can produce. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 766 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Paladin Weapon Master Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

## Hunting Strategy

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the Paladin Weapon Master Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

The Paladin Weapon Master Mouse can be found only in the Templar's Sanctum after 20 other mice have been captured. The Paladin Weapon Master Mouse can only be captured once per visit to the Templar's Sanctum.

## Loot

- Ancient Relic, Infused Plate, Plates of Fealty and Climbing Boots of Fealty.

## History and Trivia

- 9 September 2015: The Paladin Weapon Master Mouse was introduced as part of the Hollow Heights release.
- 29 August 2017: The Paladin Weapon Master Mouse began dropping Climbing Boots of Fealty in preparation for the release of Moussu Picchu.

| This mystical creature possesses an absolute mastery of the Arcane powers, allowing it to unlock some of the mysteries of the universe and even to surpass the limits of the physical body. This mighty paragon has the ability to channel and manipulate raw Arcane power through its exquisite golden staff and use it for a wide variety of purposes, many of which are nefarious. Any hunter who is weak of will should not attempt to challenge this formidable adversary because it is said that, purely with the power of thought, it can burrow into the mind and disassemble its contents from the inside out.<br>The Paragon of Arcane devotes its existence to pursuing knowledge, unravelling the universe, and obliterating anything that stands in its way.<br>"The mysteries of the Arcane are understood by few and mastered by fewer." |  |  |  |

- To encounter the Paragon of Arcane, the Hunter must pick an Arcane Keep on the Sky Map.

- To encounter the Paragon of the Lawless, the Hunter must pick a Law Garrison on the Sky Map.
- After catching the Paragon of the Lawless, the Law Garrison will not be available on subsequent Sky Maps until ​the Hunter has defeated all 4 Sky Wardens.

## Loot

- Depending on the buff present, the Paragon of the Lawless will drop the corresponding High Altitude Loot:

| Buff | High Altitude Loot |
| --- | --- |
| Paragon Bangle Shrine | Cloudstone Bangle |
| Paragon Silk Shrine | Skysoft Silk |
| Paragon Sprocket Shrine | Sky Sprocket |
| Paragon Wing Shrine | Enchanted Wing |

- Additionally, the Paragon of the Lawless can also drop the following loot:
Ancient Relic
Cyclone Stone
Divine Orb
Storm Cell

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Paragon of the Lawless was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

| Thoughtful thieves to the last, Pebble Mice have the innate ability to determine the weight of things. They spend most of their time on the vast mountainside, looking for pebbles of just the right size, shape, and weight. When they encounter a trap, they very carefully use these pebbles to replace the cheese, which fools the trap into thinking that the cheese is still there. Obviously, this tactic garners mixed results, depending on the sophistication of the trap. |  |  |  |

- 25 June 2013: The Pebble Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Pebble Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 17 October 2018: The Pebble Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice.

| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Magical genetic engineering sure has come a long way! This beefy mouse has been augmented with the strength, ferocity, and terrifying features of a genuine polar bear. As a front-line invader, he strikes fear into the heart of whomever he opposes. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 368 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Polar Bear Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro traps are of normal effectiveness against the Polar Bear Mouse.

- Slushy Shoreline

> The Polar Bear Mouse is known to drop War Scrap as loot. It also drops Cheddar Powder, but only when a Hunter is working an M400 Bait Research Assignment.

- Iceberg

> Arming a Wax Charm will increase the distance of a successful catch, and arming a Sticky Charm will reduce the distance lost from a miss.
Treacherous Tunnels
The Polar Bear Mouse drops Hunting Research (5000 points), Satchel of Gold (2000 gold), Sticky Charm and Wax Charm as loot. It also drops Cheddar Powder, but only when a Hunter is working an M400 Bait Research Assignment.
Brutal Bulwark
The Polar Bear Mouse drops Frozen Sealed Bottle, Hunting Research (5000 points), Satchel of Gold (2000 gold), Sticky Charm and Wax Charm as loot. It also drops Cheddar Powder, but only when a Hunter is working an M400 Bait Research Assignment.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2012: The Polar Bear Mouse was released in the Bazaar, Calm Clearing, Cape Clawed, Crystal Library, Great Gnarled Tree, Harbour, King's Arms, Laboratory, Meadow, Mountain, Muridae Market, S.S. Huntington II, Town of Digby, Town of Gnawnia, Training Grounds, and Windmill. Whilst the mouse was available in these locations, Draconic, Forgotten, Arcane, Shadow, Tactical, and Physical power types were listed as less effective against it, in addition to its weakness to Hydro power type.
- 7 May 2012: The mouse moved locations, and could only be found at the Slushy Shoreline where the range of power types effective against it was reduced to Hydro only.
- Between 14 May 2012 and 24 May 2012: The Polar Bear Mouse dropped Interlocking Hull Fragments.
- 1 June 2012: The Polar Bear Mouse began to drop loot in the Iceberg location.
- 18 June 2012: The Polar Bear Mouse began to drop Frozen Sealed Bottle as loot.
- 1 June 2021: The Polar Bear Mouse began to drop Teddy Bear as loot.

# 🐭 Portable Generator Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Portable Generator Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Burroughs Rift. It is known to drop Mist Canisters and The Red Button as loot.

| Portable Generator Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Rift Walkers (Burroughs Rift) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 26,500 | Gold: | 8,500 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Rift | Other Requirements: | 0 Mist |
| Cheese: | Magical String | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Burroughs Rift | Loot: | Mist CanisterThe Red Button |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Nothing is more frustrating than being on the cusp of victory when your mechanical arm runs out of juice! This has happened so many times that the rift mice have come up with a solution. The more power-hungry of the mice have been issued a companion Portable Generator Mouse to hang around with them to recharge them should they even run low on electricity.This mouse is so dedicated to its task, that the only way to distract it is to arm Magical String Cheese when there is no mist in the air. On the plus side, the energy this mouse generates comes from a form of mist fusion, so it is always in possession of a bounty of Mist Canisters when caught.Sometimes, when it thinks nobody is looking, this mouse pulls out a device with a big red button on top and stares at it long and hard. What does the button do? What is this mouse pondering while considering it? We may never know. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 657 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Portable Generator mice have been found to be especially attracted to Magical String, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Portable Generator mice are weak to Rift Traps. They can only be attracted by Magical String while hunting at 0 Mist.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Portable Generator Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg and the Rift Mist Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Portable Generator mice were introduced on 09 September 2014, with the introduction of the Burroughs Rift.

# 🐭 Present Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

> "Present" redirects here. For other uses, see Mouse of Winter Present.

The Present Mouse is an Event Mouse found during the annual MouseHunt Birthday, Lunar New Year, and Great Winter Hunt events.

| Present Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Event Mice (Birthday) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 860 | Gold: | 1,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All types | Other Requirements: | See current MouseHunt Birthday,Lunar New Year or Great Winter Hunt location |
| Cheese: | See current MouseHunt Birthday,Lunar New Year or Great Winter Hunt location | Charm: | See current MouseHunt Birthday,Lunar New Year or Great Winter Hunt location |
| Locations: | MouseHunt BirthdayLunar New YearGreat Winter Hunt | Loot: | See current MouseHunt Birthday,Lunar New Year or Great Winter Hunt location |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Celebrations are a time for gifts and feasts, and this little mouse has managed to find his way into both! A finely wrapped gift containing many rich sweets has this mouse rather occupied inside. Being that most of his senses are occupied by the inside of the box, he is using sound to navigate the world outside. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 157 |  |  |

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Present Mouse can be found on the current MouseHunt Birthday, Lunar New Year or Great Winter Hunt location page.

## History and Trivia

- The original image of the Present Mouse features a gift tag "To: Larry ♥".
- The description for the Present Mouse has changed since its release. The original description read as follows:

> When this ambitious little cheese-nabber decided to burrow his way into a Christmas present, he spent about five seconds being proud of himself for coming up with an ingenious diguise. He spent the next ten seconds figuring out that without eye holes in the box, he would end up running into walls and stepping on ornaments. Then he spent the next twenty minutes wishing that he hadn’t picked a box with a novelty trap in it.

- Great Winter Hunt '09

> 3 December 2009: The Present Mouse is released and begins dropping Icing Sugar as loot.
13 December 2009: The Present Mouse begins dropping Two Festive Mitts as loot.
18 December 2009: The Present Mouse begins dropping Seven Gingerbread Mice as loot
20 December 2009: The Present Mouse begins dropping Nine Reindeer Antlers as loot.

- MouseHunt Birthday II

> 2 March 2010: The Present Mouse begins dropping Time Out Hour Glass in the Town of Gnawnia as part of the MouseHunt Birthday II event.
7 March 2010: The Present Mouse begins dropping Stolen Birthday Gift as loot in the Town of Gnawnia.
21 March 2010: The Present Mouse begins appearing in the Great Gnarled Tree.

- 23 January 2012: The description for the Present Mouse was changed for the Year of the Dragon Festival event and read as follows:

> The Lunar New Year is a time for gifts and feasts, and this little mouse has managed to find his way into both! A finely wrapped gift containing many rich sweets has this mouse rather occupied inside. Being that most of his senses are occupied by the inside of the box, he is using sound to navigate the world outside and he seems to avoid the extremely loud fireworks. It is rumoured that a few of these packages have Nitropop potions hidden inside.

## Hunting Strategy

The Professor Morty Artie can only be attracted while writing a story.

Only Law power types are effective against Professor Morty Artie.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2026: Professor Morty Artie was released as part of Conclusion Cliffs location.

| Do not underestimate this mouse by its portly appearance and purple-flower-deelybopper headband. This mouse... is a Master of the art of puddle manipulation. ## Preferred Cheese

Puddlemancer Mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Strong Physical weapons are recommended in order to catch this mouse. Alternatively, hunters may equip their best Tactical trap.

## Event Participation

- The Puddlemancer Mouse drops the Seasonal Garden Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Puddlemancer Mice were introduced on 17 August 2010 with the release of the Seasonal Garden location.
- Puddlemancer Mice began to drop Sandtail Call to Arms on 22 March 2011 with the release of the Fiery Warpath location.
- During the Spring Egg Hunt 2011, Puddlemancer Mice began dropping a bonus Seasonal Garden Egg as loot for hunters who had obtained the Rocket Rabbit Mallet. They dropped the same loot the following year during the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event for hunters who had obtained the Eggscavator.
- On 29 July 2015 they stopped dropping the Sandtail Call to Arms as loot with the introduction of the Adventure Book.

### Cheese

The Pugilist Mouse is attracted to Standard Cheese in all its locations.

In the Town of Gnawnia location, the Pugilist Mouse is also attracted to Gilded Cheese.
In the Windmill location, the Pugilist Mouse is not attracted to Grilled Cheese.

No Charms are required to attract the Pugilist Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 June 2010: The Pugilist Mouse was introduced as part of the Windmill update.
- 25 June 2013: The Pugilist Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Pugilist Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Pugilist Mouse began dropping Packets of Flour at the Windmill.

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Pyrehyde Mouse is only attracted to Flamin' Queso.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Pyrehyde Mouse.

## Hunting Strategy

Catching a Pyrehyde Mouse while building pressure in Queso Geyser increases the amount of pressure by 100 kPa.

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Pyrehyde Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Pyrehyde Mouse can only be encountered while building pressure in Queso Geyser, after a cork has been constructed.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Pyrehyde Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2019: The Pyrehyde Mouse was introduced, with the release of the Queso Geyser area.

These mice collect and date rainwater from as far back as they can remember, passing their collections down from generation to generation.<br>"This rainwater here is from the flash flood of the great downpour of '78! - 6 September 2017: The Rain Collector Mouse was introduced as part of the Moussu Picchu release.

The Ravenous Zombie Mouse is attracted to the following cheeses:

> Undead Emmental
Undead String Emmental

- Forbidden Grove

> Ancient Cheese
Radioactive Blue Cheese

- Mousoleum

> Radioactive Blue Cheese

The Ravenous Zombie Mouse is known to drop Brain Bits in any location when hunting with the Limited Edition Brain Extractor or Maniacal Brain Extractor traps or Skello-ton base.

The Ravenous Zombie Mouse is known to drop Cemetery Slat, Wealth Charm and Super Wealth Charm in Mousoleum.

The Ravenous Zombie Mouse is not known to drop any other loot in Catacombs and Forbidden Grove.

## History and Trivia

- 21 July 2008: The Ravenous Zombie Mouse was introduced.
- 12 January 2011: The Ravenous Zombie Mouse was found at Ronza's Traveling Shoppe for the first time.
- 18 April 2011: The Ravenous Zombie Mouse began dropping the Antique Wavy Purple Egg as loot as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 26 May 2011: The Ravenous Zombie Mouse began dropping Undead Emmental Potions as loot in the Mousoleum in preparation for the Zombie Invasion Weekend.
- 27 May 2011: The Ravenous Zombie Mouse was re-introduced to the Catacombs where it began dropping Undead Emmental Potions (only during Zombie Invasion Weekends) as loot and gained a preference for Undead Emmental cheese.

In Gnawnia Rift, the Raw Diamond Mouse has been found to be especially attracted to Riftiago cheese, although Swiss String, Brie String, and Magical String cheese will also suffice.

In Furoma Rift, the Raw Diamond Mouse is only attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Raw Diamond Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg and the Gnawnia Rift Egg in the Gnawnia Rift during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

### Other Events

- 2014

> Halloween 2014

## History and Trivia

- The Raw Diamond Mouse is the Rift version of the Diamond Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Raw Diamond Mouse was introduced as part of the Rift Plane.
- 18 May 2016: The Raw Diamond Mouse was released in Furoma Rift.

| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 3,000 | Gold: | 1,440 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Arcane | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Ancient CheeseMoon CheeseRadioactive Blue | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Forbidden Grove | Loot: | Ancient RelicDivine OrbRuneRunic PotionStale CheeseWarp Nail |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Seldom seen, but often felt as a buzz in the air or the smell of ozone, Realm Ripper mice bounce between dimensions at will, revelers in time and space. Because they are free to inhabit any time period and dimension, Realm Rippers seldom form any permanent allegiances, but the Acolyte Mouse has managed to bribe a number of them to ferry his minions in and out of his prison in the Acolyte Realm. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 279 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Realm Ripper can be attracted by Ancient Cheese, Moon Cheese, and Radioactive Blue Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Arcane traps are very effective against the Realm Ripper. Forgotten and Shadow traps are of normal effectiveness.

The Realm Ripper's availability depends on the status of Forbidden Grove:

- While the Forbidden Grove is Closed, the Realm Ripper is the only mouse the Hunter can attract here.
- While the Forbidden Grove is Open, the Realm Ripper is not in the attraction pool unless the Hunter arms Realm Ripper Charm. Doing so will add the Realm Ripper and remove all other mice from the attraction pool.

Catching a Realm Ripper in Forbidden Grove will transfer the Hunter to Acolyte Realm.

## History and Trivia

- 21 September 2011: The Realm Ripper was released with the stabilization of the opening and closing of the gates to the Acolyte Realm.
- 25 January 2011: The Realm Ripper began to drop Divine Orb as loot.
- 23 August 2011: The Realm Ripper began to drop Warp Nail as loot when working on a Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.
- 29 August 2012: The Realm Ripper began to drop Ancient Relic as loot.
- 31 May 2017: The Realm Ripper Charm was introduced, making it possible to catch a Realm Ripper without having to wait for the Forbidden Grove to close.

## Finding Event Mice

Information on how to locate and capture the Red Envelope Mouse can be found on the current Lunar New Year location page.

## History and Trivia

- 12 February 2010: The Red Envelope Mouse was introduced as part of the Heart of the Tiger.
- The amount of points (888) awarded on catching a Red Envelope Mouse, and the amount of gold held in the loot drops (222 and 888), reflects the belief that the numbers eight and two are "lucky" in Chinese culture; a sequence of a "lucky" number is considered even more favorable!
- The description of the Red Envelope Mouse has had slight changes made to it since the mouse's release. The original description read as follows:

> "There is a tradition among the mice who participate in the Lunar New Year celebrations. Some mice exchange red envelopes with each other, filled with gold that they have looted from poor, unsuspecting hunters. Red boxes, red envelopes? Coincidence? We think not."

# 🐭 Relic Hunter Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Relic Hunter Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Rare Rodent) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 650 | Gold: | 935 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All Power Types except Parental | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | None | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Randomly changes every 24 hours.See Locations section below. | Loot: | Ancient Relic |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| For many years, the Relic Hunter Mouse scoured the Kingdom of Gnawnia in search of precious Ancient Relics and would steal them from unsuspecting hunters wherever she went. At long last, her search has finally completed and she has collected so many Ancient Relics that she can now undertake her ultimate goal of using them to decipher the true origin of mice in Gnawnia and why they came here; an ancient mystery that has been lost to the ages. Now she travels all around the Kingdom, studying the wonders of each place she visits, and using the Ancient Relics as the key to unlocking the secrets of murine prehistory.<br>The Relic Hunter Mouse is restless and obsessive, traveling to a new location every day, as she tries to solve her great mystery. Hunters who are looking to obtain Ancient Relics for their own purposes can attempt to decipher the clues she leaves behind to locate this elusive mouse. Once she's been located, simply hunt in that location and she'll occasionally pass by your trap. She is too preoccupied to bother with your cheese or charm but if you manage to catch her, you'll be able to loot some of her precious Ancient Relics! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 401 |  |  |

## Attracting the Relic Hunter Mouse

Each hunt in the current location of the Relic Hunter Mouse, both through sounding the Hunter's Horn and the hourly trap check, has the potential to attract the Relic Hunter Mouse in addition to any mice that may be attracted to the Hunter's Cheese.
Encountering a Relic Hunter Mouse will not affect progress in any area when encountered.

The attraction of the Relic Hunter Mouse is not affected by any Cheese or Charms.

### Bonus Hunt

A successful catch of the Relic Hunter Mouse will occur as a bonus hunt that does not consume either Cheese or Charms.
The outcome of the main hunt, whether it is a successful catch, failure to catch or failure to attract, has no impact on the outcome of the Relic Hunter Mouse's bonus hunt.

## Locations

Every 24 hours, the Relic Hunter Mouse randomly moves to one of the following locations.
There is a chance for the Relic Hunter Mouse to move to the current location it was at, effectively staying at the same location for more than 24 hours.
Viewing the Treasure Map popup under the Invites and Scroll Cases tab offers a hint to the Relic Hunter's current location.

Note: The Relic Hunter Mouse appears in the Living Garden and Twisted Garden at the same time.

| Region | Location | The Relic Hunter has been spotted... |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Gnawnia | Town of Gnawnia | Hiding within the hustle and bustle in the city of the crown.<br>In a town with a dense population.<br>Trying to spot the King himself. |
| Windmill | By an agricultural structure once owned by one of Gnawnia's most prosperous farmers.<br>Grinding up hundreds of tiny seeds from a stalky, golden plant.<br>Observing the churning and grinding of the new harvest. |  |
| Harbour | By the sea where there's plenty of fresh air and sunshine.<br>Near the loud and low horns and the dinging of bells.<br>Visiting where many new hunters seek out seafaring mice. |  |
| Mountain | Cutting through the pass to reach the town on the other side.<br>Looking across vast landscapes and the many horizons of the land.<br>In a treacherous environment where only the toughest of mice survive. |  |
| Valour | King's Arms | Browsing wares available with a most royal currency.<br>Under a circular roof atop arm-shared paths.<br>Where royal strength rewards hunting prowess. |
| Tournament Hall | Amongst the triumphant trumpets of master hunters of old.<br>Browsing the rewards of competitive champions.<br>Competing for the limelight amongst the finest champions. |  |
| King's Gauntlet | Atop a tall tower with the perfect view of an Eclipse.<br>Climbing up spiralling, menacing stairs.<br>Trekking up a massive tower in Valour. |  |
| Whisker Woods | Calm Clearing | By a peaceful rock in a grassy clearing.<br>Taking a relaxing hike through a forested area.<br>Tucked behind dense trees where it's quiet and peaceful. |
| Great Gnarled Tree | By a tree older than Gnawnia itself.<br>Finding shade in the largest tree in the Kingdom.<br>Tracing the deep patterns of bark growing on ancient towers. |  |
| Lagoon | Amongst sparkling, still water.<br>Climbing jagged rocks and slick moss.<br>Waist-deep in a shallow, sparkling pond. |  |
| Burroughs | Laboratory | Amongst brightly glowing potions.<br>Performing bizarre experiments and chemical reactions.<br>Where the powerful and strange breeds of mice first arose. |
| Town of Digby | Amongst powerful drills and excavation equipment.<br>Exploring the deep and winding caverns near a technologically-advanced underground city.<br>Hiding in the shadows while standing in the limelight. |  |
| Mousoleum | Investigating the spirits of slain mice.<br>Studying the spooky remains of Zombie Mice.<br>Surveying where scientists harvest 'spare parts'. |  |
| Bazaar | Ducking between stalls and tents and loud merchants.<br>Searching for the best deals in the Burroughs.<br>Under the pointiest tent in all the Kingdom! |  |
| Furoma | Training Grounds | Relaxing in the shade of tall engraved rock.<br>Standing among the ranks of new students out in the field.<br>Watching the careful training of artful students. |
| Dojo | Carefully watching the training activities of advanced students.<br>Practicing an ancient art with fledgling warriors.<br>Safely inside the bottom floor of a bamboo building. |  |
| Bristle Woods | Catacombs | Listening for sinister secrets deep underground.<br>Walking through dark hallways in search of a Keeper's Candle. |
| Forbidden Grove | Behind heavy stone gates.<br>Trapped between two planes of existence. |  |
| Acolyte Realm | Inside an elaborate one-way trap designed by Plankrun.<br>Outside a smoky purple tower.<br>Standing on the other side of a green and purple portal. |  |
| Tribal Isles | Cape Clawed | On a small bit of land near a volcano.<br>Watching the peaceful gathering of tribal mice. |
| Elub Shore | Near the blue waters of the island.<br>Watching the calm waters of Rodentia while remaining safely ashore. |  |
| Nerg Plains | Investigating a well-seasoned Gumbo Cheese.<br>Running through flat fields of green. |  |
| Derr Dunes | Ankle deep in rocky, tropical sand.<br>Tumbling down hills of red sand. |  |
| Balack's Cove | Lurking in a damp and darkened grotto.<br>Roaming amongst the most powerful of Lich mice. |  |
| Varmint Valley | Claw Shot City | Keeping an eye on the long-arm of the law.<br>Spitting in a spittoon! Yuck! |
| Queso Canyon | Queso River | Sipping delicious liquid cheese from a river.<br>Enjoying a quick dip in a cheesy bath.<br>Protecting her ears from the sound of loud pumps. |
| Rodentia | S.S. Huntington IV | Looking a bit queasy...<br>Testing out balance on the high seas. |
| Slushy Shoreline | At the beachside site of an invasion force!<br>Shivering near the edges of the mainland.<br>Walking along the coldest waters in Gnawnia. |  |
| Seasonal Garden | Braving the ever-changing elements.<br>Watching the sky and wondering what the weather will bring. |  |
| Crystal Library | Expanding knowledge and climbing endless ladders.<br>Leafing through ancient tomes of knowledge. |  |
| Sunken City | Investigating powerful diving equipment.<br>Walking along the bottom of the Rodentia Ocean. |  |
| Sandtail Desert | Fiery Warpath | Dodging arrows, spears, swords, and spells!<br>Marching through the Sandtail Desert. |
| Muridae Market | Keeping a close eye on would-be thieves...<br>Visiting a walled city that is no stranger to sieges. |  |
| Living GardenandTwisted Garden | Enjoying a drink on the overgrown rooftop patio.<br>Tending to a most troublesome and dangerous garden. |  |
| Hollow Heights | Fungal Cavern | Carefully navigating a subterranean and humid environment.<br>Deep inside of an infested, glowing, twisting, unending cave of untold riches... |
| Moussu Picchu | Climbing and exploring some long lost ruins.<br>Up high upon a weather changing plateau. |  |
| Floating Islands | In the clouds above Hollow Heights.<br>Investigating what can be built in a workshop.<br>Peering through an oculus.<br>Searching the skies for treasure.<br>Watching hunters' dirigibles fly by.<br>Avoiding falling victim to Sky Pirates. |  |
| Cerulean Skyport | Parleying with Sky Raiders.<br>Unloading trade goods from airships. |  |
| Folklore Forest | Foreword Farm | Reaping what she sowed...<br>Cultivating a hearty yield...<br>Toiling away in fertile fields... |
| Prologue Pond | Casting a line...<br>Reeling it in...<br>Choosing the right tackle... |  |
| Table of Contents | Sitting down at a wooden table...<br>Creating clever characters...<br>Penning plot points...<br>Sharpening a quill... |  |
| Bountiful Beanstalk | Climbing into the clouds.<br>Hobnobbing with giants.<br>Infiltrating a lofty castle. |  |
| School of Sorcery | Studying for finals.<br>Learning new tricks.<br>Getting an education. |  |
| Draconic Depths | Descending into dangerous depths.<br>Braving the elements below.<br>Navigating through dark, underground caverns. |  |
| Afterword Acres | Getting splinters from woodworking.<br>Sitting under the Infinitree.<br>Carving her name in a tree. |  |
| Epilogue Falls | Riding in a wooden barrel.<br>Swimming against the current. |  |
| Conclusion Cliffs |  |  |

## History and Trivia

- 29 August 2012: The Relic Hunter Mouse is released in the Muridae Market. Its location changed on a predictable weekly basis.
- 6 May 2013: The daily location of Relic Hunter Mouse mouse begins to change randomly.
- 28 April 2013: Catching the Relic Hunter Mouse in the Seasonal Garden begins giving hunters 1% increase in their Tower Amplifier.
- 23 June 2015: The Relic Hunter Mouse stopped dropping Treasure Map Scroll Cases and Living Garden Scroll Cases and began dropping Relic Hunter Scroll Cases as part of Season 3 of treasure hunting.
- 27 July 2016: The Relic Hunter Mouse encounter was changed to a Bonus Hunt that does not consume cheese or charms. The second paragraph of its description was also changed. The previous description read:

> This mouse is so dedicated to the study of Ancient Relics that they'll never approach your trap until you have at least one, and will approach more often if you have more. Further, the Relic Hunter Mouse will only be searching in one location at a time, so you'll have to search if you expect to find any!

- 13 November 2024: The Relic Hunter Mouse has been modified to drop Ancient Relics instead of stealing them in exchange for Relic Hunter Scroll Cases.

> As a result, Hunters no longer require Ancient Relics in their inventory in order to attract the Relic Hunter Mouse.
The description was also changed to reflect this change. The old description read:
From the mountains of Gnawnia to Zugzwang's famous Gardens, the Relic Hunter Mouse leaves no trap unturned in the search for elusive Ancient Relics. Sure, they can be found in the possession of some of the most dangerous mice around, but, well... That's dangerous! It's far safer (relatively) to try to check out the relics that hunters have accrued. And besides, free cheese!

> This mouse is so dedicated to the study of Ancient Relics that she'll never approach your trap until you have at least one. The Relic Hunter Mouse will stay in one location for an entire day before moving on, so you'll have to search Gnawnia to find her every day!

# 🐭 Retired Minotaur Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Retired Minotaur Mouse can be found in Zokor. It is known to drop a very large variety of loot.

| Retired Minotaur Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Citizens of Zokor (Hidden Remnant) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,000,000 | Gold: | 1,000,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Forgotten | Other Requirements: | 30 Fealty, 30 Scholar, 30 Tech clues and a Minotaur Key. |
| Cheese: | None | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Zokor | Loot: | Ancient RelicBaitkeep CharmCavern FungusDiamondDiamond Boost CharmDivine OrbEnigmatic CoreEssence of DestructionFlawless OrbGemstoneGemstone Boost CharmLantern OilNightshadeNightshade Farming CharmPlate of FealtyReally, Really Shiny Precious GoldScholar ScrollScrap MetalShuffler's CubeSimple OrbTech Power CoreTemporal Shadow PlateUltimate Attraction CharmUltimate CharmUltimate Luck CharmUltimate Lucky Power CharmUltimate Power CharmUltimate Spore CharmUnstable Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Once young and lively, this minotaur loved racing through the hallways of the Labyrinth, chasing down hunters and mice alike, howling and bellowing with delight as they scurried at the mere sound of his hooves on the cobbled floors. But now, it seems he has run his last lap of the maze, and has settled down into cozy end-life routines. Donning his fuzzy slippers in his favourite easy chair (which is a giant sack of gold), this retired beast has had his pension paid out in full and has relocated to his secret inner chamber to enjoy his retirement in peace and quiet.<br>The same shenanigans that used to bring him joy now only cause him grief, and he can be seen shaking his fist from atop his tower at the other mice hustling and bustling in the city. All that noise, all that racket! What a grumpy old grump this once playful beast has become...<br>But even in his twilight and seemingly endless misery, he still manages to let a small grin spread across his lips whenever he causes a mouse to jump in fear at the sound of his snort or the clipping of his hooves.<br>No one has broken into his private chambers and lived to tell the tale. Rumor has it that he stores many great and valuable items on opulent displays. Perhaps a resourceful hunter can find a way in and nab one of these rare treasures before being caught! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 782 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Retired Minotaur Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

## Hunting Strategy

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the Retired Minotaur Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

The Retired Minotaur Mouse can only be found in the Lair of the Minotaur. While it can be encountered at any time within the Lair of the Minotaur after the Hunter has caught any one mouse, it is more easily attracted and caught as its state (as indicated in the HUD) moves closer to Reckless. Hunters may only catch one Retired Minotaur Mouse per visit to the Lair of the Minotaur.

Hunters are returned to the beginning of the Labyrinth upon catching a Retired Minotaur Mouse.

## Loot

The Retired Minotaur Mouse will drop a variety of loot from the following list:

- Charms

Diamond Boost Charm
Gemstone Boost Charm
Nightshade Farming Charm
Ultimate Attraction Charm
Ultimate Charm
Ultimate Luck Charm
Ultimate Lucky Power Charm

Unstable Charm

- Crafting Items

## History and Trivia

8 September 2020: The Retired Minotaur Mouse no longer drops Forgotten Charms

## Cheese Preference

The Richard the Rich is attracted to Cloud Cheesecake.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Island Based Loot

When found on a Low Altitude Island or a High Altitude Island, depending on the buffs present, the Richard the Rich can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

Richard the Rich also drops Storm Cells outside of the Sky Palace, if Rocket Boosters have been attached on the Hunter's Dirigible. It also has a chance to drop the following loot:

- High Altitude Treasure Trove
- Low Altitude Treasure Trove
- Richard's Sky Yacht Airship Sail
- Richard's Sky Yacht Airship Balloon
- Richard's Sky Yacht Airship Hull

## Hunting Strategy

All powertypes except Rift and Parental are effective against the Richard the Rich. The Richard the Rich can only be encountered on an island with 2 Loot Caches present and active.

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Richard the Rich was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

# 🐭 Riptide Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Riptide Mouse is a breed of mouse found in Balack's Cove.

Riptide mice cannot be found during the low and medium tides in Balack's Cove; they only appear during high tides. Upon capturing this mouse, hunters are transported to Jungle of Dread.

| Riptide Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Balack's Banished |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 32,000 | Gold: | 3,800 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | ShadowArcaneForgotten | Other Requirements: | See Hunting Strategy below |
| Cheese: | Vanilla StiltonVengeful Vanilla Stilton | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Balack's Cove | Loot: | Flawless OrbScrap Metal |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Before it was home to a swarm of lich fiends, Balack’s Cove was known for its strange tidal patterns. One moment, the tide is so far out to sea that you can see clams going about their business a mile out, and the next, it rushes back in, swallowing anything caught in the spillover. The source of these tidal quirks is the Riptide Mouse, a mercurial character who is seldom seen, but often felt when its waves wash hapless Hunters up on the sandy shores of the Jungle of Dread. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 165 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Riptide mice have been found to be attracted to both Vanilla Stilton cheese and Vengeful Vanilla Stilton cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Capturing a Riptide Mouse will cause Hunters to be washed out of Balack's Cove and into the Jungle of Dread.

- During Low Tide and Medium Tide the Riptide Mouse cannot be encountered.
- During High Tide the Riptide Mouse is the only mouse that can be encountered with its preferred cheese.

Arming any of the various Anchor Charms will prevent a Hunter from being washed out when capturing a Riptide Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 17 January 2023: Riptide Mouse stopped appearing on all Treasure Maps. Riptide Mouse also began to drop Flawless Orb and Scrap Metal as loot.

When found in an Empyrean Sky Palace Vault, depending on the buffs present, the Rocketeer Mouse can drop the following loot:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass and Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Ore and Glass Deposits and Loot Caches |
| Corsair's Curd | Drops when a Pirate Den is present and active Can be boosted by Loot Caches or additional Pirate Dens |
| Raw Ancient Jade | Drops when an Ancient Jade Stockpile is present and active Can be boosted by Loot Caches or additional Ancient Jade Stockpiles |

The Rouge O'Hair can only be attracted while writing a story.

Only Law power types are effective against Rouge O'Hair.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2026: Rouge O'Hair was released as part of Conclusion Cliffs location.

## Preferred Cheese

The RR-8 Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere in both the Labyrinth and Zokor, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

## Hunting Strategy

Forgotten weapons are very effective against the RR-8 Mouse. Arcane weapons are of normal effectiveness.

- Labyrinth

> The RR-8 Mouse can be found in Plain, Superior, and Epic Tech Hallways.

- Zokor

> The RR-8 Mouse can be found in the Tech Foundry Outskirts, the Tech Research Center, and the Manaforge.

- Zokor

> Labyrinth Theme Scrap I and Tech Power Cores
